A physically viable pulse system need not maintain a static field indefinitely. It **does** need a lawful, controlled waveform for each pulse and a repeatable bounded complete-system operating regime. A field can be useful for a finite time even if a static equilibrium does not exist **if** it can be established before the problematic mode grows, and if repeated cycling does not accumulate an instability or hidden energy.

**What can be relaxed, subject to proof:** indefinite static equilibrium; permanent static confinement; absence of all *static-background* instabilities when a finite-time valid trajectory exists; continuous instantaneous 1g (only under the separately labelled cycle-average engineering metric). A genuinely unstable transient can be usable only if start/stop/reset avoids damage and repeated-cycle instability. **Do not relax:** healthy propagating mode kinetic signs, hyperbolic evolution, gauge/Ward/Noether identities, microscopic conserved driving, universal metric, finite matter loading, momentum reaction, empirical limits and total energy.

The simplest duty-cycle bound is illustrative: if useful acceleration is $a_{\rm on}$ for fraction $D$ and zero otherwise, $\overline a=D a_{\rm on}$. At $D=0.5,0.1,0.01$, ideal peak values of 2g, 10g, and 100g respectively are needed for an average 1g. This ignores negative-force phases, material stress, and source energy, so it is **not** a prediction.

**Repeat-cycle requirements:**

- Consecutive pulses evolve from previous final fields and matter states; no forced reinitialization to unloaded R3B.
- After startup, cycle-average force is stable and reproducible, not merely a transient first-pulse effect.
- Track peak stress, cumulative heating, material/interface modes, nonaxisymmetric perturbations, radiation, field leakage and active control.
- Evaluate Floquet/parametric growth or equivalent bounded-periodic stability, including the historical 018C warning for $m=2$ modes.
- Refine temporal and spatial discretization, simulation domain and source boundaries independently; repeat sufficient cycles to distinguish saturation from delayed divergence.

**Promotion metric:** stable average $\overline F_{\rm payload}\ge M g$ is a new `PULSED_AVERAGE_FINITE_PAYLOAD` milestone, not the same as the original static/local everywhere-1g milestone. Whole-craft lift requires its own external reaction proof.

## 12. Whole-craft reaction, gravity cancellation and spacecraft feasibility

**Payload force is not total vehicle thrust.** If the source and payload are bolted to one isolated vehicle, their mutual interaction contributes zero net center-of-mass impulse except for momentum exported by field flux or transferred to an external object. For full GR/coupled metric theories, use the appropriate covariant total stress-energy/flux statement rather than interpreting a coordinate or local force as a global device thrust.

The spacecraft-oriented decisive ledger is:

```text
PAYLOAD_UPWARD_IMPULSE
SOURCE_DOWNWARD_OR_OTHER_REACTION_IMPULSE
SUPPORT_LOADS_AND_TRANSFER
FIELD_MOMENTUM_CHANGE
EXPORTED_RADIATION_MOMENTUM
EXTERNAL_EARTH_COUPLING_AND_EXCHANGED_IMPULSE
TOTAL_SOURCE_PLUS_PAYLOAD_PLUS_SUPPORTS_CENTER_OF_MASS_IMPULSE
```

A physically allowed external Earth interaction **could** provide the reaction partner if independently derived. It has not yet been shown to exist in the proposed R3B model. A rotating or pulsed internal source cannot simply cancel Earth's gravity on itself by re-labeling an internal force.

Keep these claim levels separate:

```text
EXTERNAL_PAYLOAD_PULSE_FORCE
FULLY_LOADED_FINITE_NEUTRAL_PAYLOAD_IMPULSE
SUSTAINED_CYCLE_AVERAGED_FINITE_PAYLOAD_LIFT
WHOLE_CRAFT_NET_EXTERNAL_EARTH_REFERENCED_LIFT
FULL_SOURCED_SAME_ACTION_MODEL
INDEPENDENT_EXPERIMENTAL_GRAVITATIONAL_REPULSION
```

Only the last classes could plausibly support a spacecraft engineering decision. The earlier classes remain valuable scientific advances if reproduced.

## 13. Pulse energy, source power, recycling, and strict practicality

**Never equate the 1.542916 J preloaded canonical inventory with source work or propulsion energy.** The field can be continuously replenished without consuming its whole inventory per cycle, but it still must supply useful mechanical work, losses and reaction in a balanced energy budget.

For the hypothetical **full field inventory irreversibly lost** each cycle,

```math
P_{\rm rebuild}=f E_{\rm lost}\quad\text{with}\quad E_{\rm lost}=1.5429162116460216\ {\rm J}
```

Conditional examples: 1 kHz $\approx1.54$ kW, 1 MHz $\approx1.54$ MW, 300 MHz $\approx463$ MW. **These are illustrative full-loss costs, not measured required pulse frequencies or power.** A resonator with verified energy-recovery fraction could have smaller dissipative loss; its actual $Q$, instability, driver and material costs are unknown.

A 1-kg body lifted 1 m against approximately constant 1g gravitational weight gains $\sim9.80665$ J gravitational potential energy; this mechanical work need not equal the instantaneous carrier field inventory. General motion requires $W_{\rm mech}=\int\mathbf F\cdot d\mathbf x$ and a mission-specific energy ledger.

**Separate** initial build/activation/field inventory, per-cycle energy input, recovered energy, irreversible loss, peak power, average power, off-state load, material source rest/internal energy, and mechanical work. The strict $<10$ MJ complete operating-energy requirement needs an explicitly defined **operating interval/motion protocol**, as dissipative continuous operation over unlimited time cannot have a finite cumulative energy budget. Preserve the strict threshold; do not change it to a field-only or arbitrarily short-time budget.

Include all unavoidable physical components without double counting:

```text
SOURCE_REST_AND_INTERNAL_ENERGY
SOURCE_CONFINEMENT/SUPPORT/STRUCTURAL_STRESSES
DRIVING_CIRCUITRY_AND_CHARGE_TRANSPORT
CANONICAL_ELECTRIC/MAGNETIC_AND_DYNAMIC_FIELD_ENERGY
METRIC_AND_COMPENSATOR_ENERGY
STARTUP/ACTIVATION/SHUTDOWN/RESET
RECOVERED_ENERGY_AND_IRREVERSIBLE_LOSS
RADIATION/LEAKAGE/HEATING
PAYLOAD_AND_SOURCE_REACTION
MECHANICAL_WORK
BACKREACTION_AND_OFF_STATE
QUANTUM/RADIATIVE/UV_COSTS
```

If one rigorously unavoidable nonnegative component is $\ge10$ MJ for the declared protocol, close **that practical realization**. If only a small partial energy is known, report `COMPLETE_OPERATING_ENERGY=UNKNOWN`, not PASS.

## 14. Microscopic sources, healthy modes, parity/CP, empirical and UV gates

A time-dependent **effective** source at the 2-m boundary is not yet a microscopic source. Required before a physical-model claim: actual charges and currents; continuity; finite numbers/densities/materials; voltages and source work; support and reaction forces; electromagnetic/hidden-sector couplings; source selectivity and neutral payload universality; source energy and cooling. The R3X3B2 minimal massive axial Dirac theorem and R3X3B3 electron-dipole floor remain closed in scope.

The $F\widetilde F$ portal is pseudoscalar under conventional parity. A universal conformal physical metric with a pseudoscalar coefficient is not automatically parity/CP-consistent. Derive a specific symmetry transformation or explicitly parity-breaking model and match its phenomenology. An axion $aF\widetilde F$ term by itself does **not** cure R3X3B5's exact-field-preservation obstruction and introduces a physical scalar with its own source and stability requirements.

A temporary pulse may **relax the requirement of permanent static equilibrium**, but it cannot make a ghost kinetic mode, uncontrolled superluminal/ill-posed equation, anomaly or broken conservation physically healthy merely by switching it off quickly. Check the full gauge-fixed physical fluctuation spectrum, canonical Hamiltonian, constraints, hyperbolicity and nonlinear material stability. Identify fixed-background instabilities that can be outrun versus nonphysical degrees of freedom that cannot be excused.

Empirical/UV review must match the **actual physical operator**, not generic “axion” or “fifth-force” bounds by name. Include: electromagnetic environmental fields, equivalence principle, inverse-square-law tests, laboratory force balances, photon mixing, spin-dependent effects, stellar losses, radiative generation of lower-dimension operators, cutoff and technical naturalness at the very low portal normalization. A pulse waveform does not automatically evade static or time-averaged constraints. A successful PDE does not certify experimentally allowable couplings.

| **P7** | Independent physical-model reproduction and engineering feasibility | All physical requirements and externally referenced whole-craft interaction verified | Only now consider prototype-design work; experiment required for physical proof |

Do not perform expensive time-domain toroidal solves before P1 and P2. A cheap causal *single pulse* can be run after the exact constraints are satisfied, even if infinite-duration static equilibrium is nonexistent. Existing static BVP restrictions are not automatically restrictions on valid transient initial-value problems, but physical source and conservation gates still apply.

11. Record one new scientific journal, new chronology notes, hashes/versions, JSON and fresh test counts. Regenerate codebundle and journal bundle using their designated scripts. Update README only for independently supported physical claim levels.

**Preferred delivery for code/run work:** complete paste-ready Bash blocks directly in chat, distinct **apply** and **verify** commands, no git commands in run instructions and no download-only script deliverables unless requested. Preserve original artifacts and filenames.

# 0. Purpose and authority

This document controls **future scientific decisions**, not chronological narration.

Its job is to answer:

```text
WHAT IS THE STRONGEST CURRENT SCIENTIFIC STATE?

WHAT EXACTLY IS THE ACTIVE MECHANISM?

WHAT HAS ALREADY BEEN PROVED OR FALSIFIED?

WHAT MUST BE FROZEN?

WHAT IS THE CHEAPEST DECISIVE NEXT TEST?

WHAT RESULT KILLS THE CURRENT REALIZATION?

WHAT RESULT PROMOTES IT?

WHEN DO WE MOVE TO THE NEXT SOURCE FAMILY?

WHEN IS A NEW PDE/BVP ACTUALLY JUSTIFIED?

WHEN IS ENERGY WORK ACTUALLY JUSTIFIED?

WHAT MUST NEVER BE REOPENED WITHOUT NEW PHYSICS?

WHAT WOULD ALLOW A12 TO SUCCEED 006D?
```

The buildplan should remain **shorter and more operational** than the journal and failure-memory documents.

Repository roles remain:

```text
LATEST COMPLETED RESULT ARTIFACTS / RUN OUTPUT
    implementation-level scientific truth

LATEST CODEBUNDLE
    implementation reconstruction

RESEARCH_BUILDPLAN.md
    current strategy and decision tree

AGMINER_BUILDPLAN.MD
    theorem-first miner architecture and failure memory

INTROSPECTIVE_BUILDPLAN.md
    mechanism knowledge and source-efficiency anatomy

NOTES.md / chronology notes
    carry-forward state

journal/
    durable proofs, falsifications, derivations, claim boundaries

README.md
    conservative public state

FORMATTING_AND_CODE_STANDARDS.md
    implementation and reporting rules
```

When documents conflict:

```text
NEWEST COMPLETED RESULT / RUN
>
NEWEST NOTES
>
NEWEST JOURNAL
>
CURRENT CODE
>
RESEARCH_BUILDPLAN
>
AGMINER_BUILDPLAN
>
README
>
OLDER DOCUMENTS
```

Material frontier changes should update the buildplan.

Minor candidate tests should not trigger a complete strategic rewrite.

Do not spread one source family across many sequential runs if the calculations are cheap and logically coupled.

The optimal unit of work is:

If any exact theorem establishes:

```text
WARD FAILURE

SOURCE INVENTORY >=10 MJ

UV CUTOFF BELOW REQUIRED DEVICE SCALE

UNAVOIDABLE LARGE PHOTON MIXING

STELLAR / LAB BOUND ORDERS OF MAGNITUDE TOO STRONG

SOURCE SUPPORT FLOOR >=10 MJ

OR
DIRECT NONMETRIC PAYLOAD FORCE INCONSISTENT WITH EXPERIMENT
```

then:

```text
CLOSE THE MINIMAL ELECTRON PAULI REALIZATION
```

and immediately advance to the next A12D0-ranked source family.

Do not spend multiple rescue runs trying to preserve Pauli unless a genuinely new symmetry, composite enhancement, or operator completion changes the theorem.

```text
A12B MASSLESS GAUGE DIRECTION
```

# 31. New-BVP authorization rule

A new PDE/BVP is authorized only after the candidate source satisfies:

```text
SOURCE EXISTS

EXACT CURRENT/WARD CONSISTENCY

CANONICAL SOURCE NORMALIZATION

SAME-ACTION PROVENANCE AT REQUIRED ORDER

NO FATAL EMPIRICAL BOUND

NO FATAL NATURALNESS / UV BOUND

NO FATAL SOURCE-ENERGY LOWER BOUND

NO FATAL SUPPORT LOWER BOUND
```

Then ask:

```text
DOES THE MICROSCOPIC SOURCE PROFILE MATCH THE FROZEN A12C KERNEL?
```

If yes:

```text
REUSE / LOAD THE EXISTING KERNEL FIRST
```

and only perform a fresh BVP if source backreaction or source morphology materially changes the solution.

This rule should save substantial compute and session time.

# 42. Candidate ranking principle

Do not rank only by theoretical elegance or raw coupling.

A high-value candidate has:

```text
EXACT CONSERVATION

HIGH CANONICAL SOURCE/J

STRONG SOURCE SELECTIVITY

LOW EMPIRICAL EXPOSURE

HIGH A12C KERNEL REUSE

LOW SUPPORT TAX

LOW UV SCAFFOLDING

NATURAL PROTECTION

NO ORDINARY PAYLOAD DIRECT FORCE
```

A candidate requiring a completely new PDE and a completely new carrier should rank below one that reuses A12B/A12C unless its source physics is substantially better.

This is how the project should preserve today's unusually high research efficiency.

Heuristics are strictly non-probabilistic.

Do not use them to choose between candidates.

Parity/CP of the pseudoscalar invariant, anomaly cancellation, dynamics, field normalization and physical-current support must all be evaluated. Merely calling a sector “axial” does not establish a viable axial gauge current.

### 5.2 P0 — baseline source and reproducibility gate (first)

Before new compensator physics, independently reproduce the R3B baseline using pinned historical source. Audit the large preloaded electric-source residual outside the permitted source support. Test whether it is caused by numerical boundary treatment, finite-grid continuation, source-mask definition or a genuine exact-source limitation.

If the exact original R3B target cannot be sourced with the permitted physical currents, **do not discard its field-capacity clue**—demote it to an *inverse-response design target* and require the new candidate to solve baseline sourcing and interface loading together. Do not hide baseline residuals inside a supposed compensator.

**Pass:** source-support map, Maxwell/Noether continuity, physical normalization and independently reconstructed energy/action response all documented.  
**Hold:** essential source localization or its physical meaning remains unresolved.  
**Fail for exact-old-field realization:** unavoidable out-of-support source under the original restricted action.

### 5.3 A — same-action Euler–Lagrange and reciprocity gate

Derive the source, carrier, metric, matter and compensator equations **by varying the same action**. Recover the corrected R3X1 Lorentzian signs and matter-gradient terms in the appropriate limit.

Check the second-variation/Helmholtz reciprocity condition using the **correct weighted discretization** (lesson of R3C1); derive the exact Noether identity for energy–momentum exchange, the source-current Ward identities and each gauge Bianchi identity. No by-hand cancellation of $\nabla T$ is permitted.

**Immediate RED:** field equations cannot arise from one common action, fail Ward/Noether, or cancel the reciprocal matter loading while independently retaining a force law.

### 5.4 B — physically different interface mechanism

Use the R3X3B0 $\Delta J_\phi,\Delta J_A$ residual as an **inverse diagnostic**. Separate free external charge/current, induced bound material polarization and compensator-gradient source terms. Verify real source support and reaction, and determine whether new terms preserve $\nabla\sigma$ across the whole finite payload.

**Immediate RED:** source term is inserted arbitrarily, demands independently controlled currents inside the nominally neutral payload, or is only a signed-sector field relabeling of R3X3B0.

**Allowed in principle but not proven:** material-bound polarization currents **derived from the complete neutral matter action**. They must include source reaction, stress-energy, conservation, stability and energy costs. Their presence is not a license for composition-dependent nonmetric forces.

### 5.5 C — parity/CP, protected gauge mode and health

For every candidate derive:

```text
COMPLETE GAUGE TRANSFORMATIONS AND EXACT CURRENT WARD
PARITY / CP ASSIGNMENTS AND PHYSICAL COMPLETION
ANOMALIES AND MASS GENERATION WHERE APPLICABLE
CANONICAL KINETIC MATRIX AND SOURCE/METRIC COUPLINGS
HAMILTONIAN POSITIVITY / GHOST / GRADIENT / TACHYON GATES
FIELD-REDEFINITION-INVARIANT OBSERVABLES
FINITE-MATTER SECOND VARIATION AND RECIPROCITY
EFT CUTOFF / UNAVOIDABLE QUANTUM OPERATORS
```

The A12B exact massless protected corridor remains an anchor but does **not** automatically protect a new pseudoscalar, axial current, polarization medium or extra field from loops and anomalies.

### 5.6 D — cheap empirical and energy falsifiers

Match the **actual canonical operator** against laboratory and astrophysical limits, including ambient electric/magnetic fields, fifth-force/equivalence-principle tests, stellar limits, photon mixing and spin-dependent interactions where applicable. Do not import bounds from superficially similar but inequivalent fields.

Estimate the **optimistic unavoidable** source, material, compensator-gradient, support and confinement energy *before* the next BVP. If any necessary nonnegative component alone is $\ge10$ MJ, close that **practical implementation**; exactly 10 MJ also fails. Otherwise label its ledger incomplete, not successful.

### 5.7 E — minimal constructive response; authorize a BVP only afterward

If the action, symmetry, source support, health, empirical and energy gates survive, test whether the new action creates a nonredefinable direction in the loaded interface response matrix, rather than a rescaling of the old shell-source space. Check the worst payload points and inverse-residual eigenmodes, not just COM force.

**RED:** fatal exact theorem, nonintegrable cancellation, gauge/Noether failure, compulsory $\ge10$ MJ physical cost, fatal instability/empirical conflict, or mere reparametrization of closed sources.  
**YELLOW:** consistent action ingredients exist but source/normalization/health/energy or finite-payload effectiveness is unresolved.  
**GREEN PRE-FLIGHT ONLY:** explicit same action, conserved and supported source, nonredefinable useful interface response, healthy canonical modes, and no known fatal cost or empirical contradiction. **Then** authorize one focused fully loaded BVP. This is not yet a physical antigravity success.

## 6. Vacuum and dilute-payload idea — controlled test, not a loophole

**Question:** Can an evacuated source/field volume eliminate unwanted *environmental* matter loading, and what is the true finite-payload test-body limit of the R3B universal-metric response?

Electric and magnetic fields can occupy vacuum. The ideal matter-free region has no ordinary material trace or trace gradient. This can remove **that specific local material contribution** to the topological field-equation loading. It does not remove field sources, chamber walls, support costs or the contribution of the **neutral payload itself**.

A 1-kg neutral torus inside a perfect vacuum still possesses material stress-energy and density interfaces. The very backreaction diagnosed by R3C/R3X3B0 therefore reappears. A no-payload vacuum field does not establish 1g on a finite object.

### Authorized diagnostic after P0/action audit

Use the same physical metric, source support and corrected Lorentzian equations. Compare:

```text
A. EMPTY_EVACUATED_ACTIVE_REGION — field only; no payload-force claim
B. DILUTE_NEUTRAL_TEST_BODY — controlled eta -> 0 physical matter limit
C. FINITE_LOWER_MASS_PAYLOADS — record actual masses and material properties
D. FULL_REFERENCE_1KG_TORUS — unchanged required benchmark
E. SMOOTH_DENSITY_OR_HOLLOW_PAYLOAD — only if fixed-mass EOS and source/support valid
```

Scaling $T_{\mu\nu}^{(\eta)}=\eta T_{\mu\nu}^{(1)}$ is a diagnostic of changing **physical matter stress-energy and typically payload mass**, not a free reduction of the fundamental metric coupling. If the model predicts a good test-body limit but fails at 1 kg, preserve the former as a separate milestone, not a practical success.

Record minimum sampled acceleration, COM acceleration, worst-point migration, canonical field energy, source/support residuals, matter gradients, source-to-payload gap and independent full solver agreement. Treat chamber walls, interfaces, source materials, time-dependent insertion, radiation and reaction explicitly if the architecture becomes serious. Do not misidentify ordinary electromagnetic acceleration of charged test particles as gravitational response.

**Vacuum branch status:** worthwhile *bounded diagnostic*, but not the next replacement for the R3X3B1 common-action preflight. No vacuum rescue has been demonstrated.

## 8. Mandatory post-BVP gates and strict complete-energy policy

Even a candidate reaching 1g / 1m in a loaded calculation must establish:

- **One full action:** source, matter, carrier, compensator, metric and gravitational sectors with exact energy–momentum exchange and diffeomorphism/Noether identities.
- **Physical source:** net-neutral finite payload, actual conserved source currents, finite source support, real material constituents and reaction forces; no fictitious internal free current.
- **Healthy modes and interacting stability:** no ghosts, gradient or nonaxisymmetric instabilities, bounded static and dynamic material/interface modes. Remember the fatal 018C $m=2$ warning.
- **Canonical and representation invariance:** no near-singular denominator gain, no fake field-basis escape, no gauge-symmetry violations.
- **Naturalness/UV/empirics:** genuine anomaly/CP completion, cutoff, radiative mixing and generated lower-dimensional operators; match the *actual* canonical coupling to environmental EM, precision gravity, fifth-force, equivalence-principle, spin, stellar and other relevant tests.
- **Nonlinear consistency:** field/source/payload gravitational backreaction, physical-metric response, reaction/radiation and conservation, not just linearized or prescribed acceleration.
- **Complete operating energy:** source and material inventory, field/mediator/metric, compensator and symmetry breaking, support/confinement, polarization, activation/reset/control, cooling, off-state, leakage, reaction/radiation, source/payload backreaction, quantum/RG/UV and unavoidable standby costs.

```math
E_{\mathrm{complete,conservative,operating}}<10^7\,\mathrm J
```

Exactly $10^7$ J fails. Any verified necessary nonnegative energy component $\ge10$ MJ closes that practical realization. A bound below 10 MJ on one component proves only headroom, not complete certification. Do not perform field-only optimization below 100 J solely for a smaller number.

## 13. Detailed physics backbone: what the R3B calculation actually calculates

### 13.1 Metric response and the canonical field inventory

A12C established a reduced gauge-invariant physical-metric route. The R3 series replaces the quadratic even-parity field-strength scalar with a **linear topological pseudoscalar response** and a mixed-electric/magnetic configuration. Schematically,

\[
\tilde g_{\mu\nu}=e^{2\sigma}g_{\mu\nu},\qquad
\sigma=\frac{F_{\alpha\beta}\tilde F^{\alpha\beta}}{2M_X^4},
\qquad F_{\alpha\beta}\tilde F^{\alpha\beta}=-4\mathbf E\cdot\mathbf B.
\]

The normalizations are the project's conventions, not a verified UV theory. In the weak, static, slow-motion limit of a universal conformal physical metric, a model acceleration has the approximate form

\[
\mathbf a\simeq -c^2\nabla\sigma,
\]

with the sign and frame conventions to be reconstructed from the full physical metric. A directly applied \(-c^2\nabla\sigma\) with the matter action omitted is **not** a completed gravitational solution.

The modeled canonical electromagnetic inventory is

\[
U_{\rm EM}=\int_{\rm domain}\frac{\lvert\mathbf E\rvert^2+\lvert\mathbf B\rvert^2}{2}\,d^3x
\]

in the adopted converted natural-unit normalization. It excludes the energy of realizing fields, compensator dynamics, support structures and other sectors. The R3B number is not a joule-to-force conversion rule or an experimentally established field effect.

### 13.2 Local topological capacity identity

At an ideal local level, use the original A12C magnetic profile \(\mathbf B_A\) and assign

\[
\mathbf E=-\frac{\mathbf B_A}{\sqrt2},\qquad
\mathbf B=+\frac{\mathbf B_A}{\sqrt2}.
\]

Then \(-4\mathbf E\cdot\mathbf B=2B_A^2\), so the proposed topological conformal factor reproduces the old quadratic-invariant metric source in the stated conventions. The sum of canonical \(E^2\) and \(B^2\) equals the original field's squared amplitude. This is a **field-level invariant identity**; static Maxwell integrability, source support, full stress-energy and finite-matter response still have to be established.

### 13.3 Global R3B completion

R3B used an external scalar magnetic potential \(\mathbf B=-\nabla\Psi\) with an exterior electrostatic mirror potential \(\Phi_{\mathrm{out}}=-\Psi/\sqrt2\), followed by regular harmonic continuation into the source sphere to avoid unnecessary electric energy. A monopole-free multipole expansion gives zero total electric charge in the declared sense. The field-level construction is predominantly dipolar.

The reported original R3B fine-grid capacity is

```text
BRANCH = 032H17A12D1R3B
R3B_NORMALIZED_PRELOADED_FIELD_ENERGY_J = 1.5429162116460216
R3B_WHOLE_PAYLOAD_MIN_ACCEL_M_S2 = 9.80665
R3B_REFERENCE_PAYLOAD_KG = 1.0
R3B_TRUE_EXTERNAL_STANDOFF_M = 1.0
R3B_NET_ELECTRIC_MONOPOLE = ZERO
R3B_LEDGER_CLASS = PRELOADED_FIELD_ONLY_CAPACITY
R3B_MICROSCOPIC_SOURCE_PHYSICALIZED = FALSE
```

The precise numerical output must remain frozen. However, a source-free exterior solution and monopole cancellation do **not** constitute a finite support-localized physical charge/current construction. Global field matching, energy integration, boundary distributions, source work and current conservation require their own independent tests.

### 13.4 Field response versus acceleration feasibility

No physical gravitational acceleration was experimentally measured. The numerical acceleration derives from the assumed nonstandard universal physical metric. Even a positive, inward/outward acceleration in the reduced model is not a complete solution if the same matter density backreacts on the carrier field and changes its configuration.

R3B reproduces a **preloaded** metric-gradient capacity; R3X3B0 shows the **loaded** same mirror is not viable at its 1g floor. These statements are simultaneously true and must always be reported together.

### 13.5 Why the topological interaction changes the loading problem

A constant coefficient of an Abelian \(F\tilde F\) density is locally a total derivative, subject to appropriate boundary assumptions. A coefficient depending on matter through the physical-metric variation can have nonzero gradients, especially near the edge of a finite payload. The R3A comparison suppressed the constant-trace bulk loading by about \(1.836\times10^{15}\), but did not remove the interface-gradient terms.

A crucial consistency rule: the same functional dependence that drives the metric response also fixes the reciprocal backreaction via the matter action. Cancelling only the latter by hand while retaining the former is not a valid model.

### 14.1 R3C1 quantified sign loss

Directly sampled *outward-to-inward* sign-loss brackets in the historical R3C1 setup occurred at approximately \(1.76418\times10^{-5}\) (0.075 m), \(1.18491\times10^{-5}\) (0.050 m) and \(1.03961\times10^{-5}\) (0.040 m). Their original sign convention and discretization must not be mixed with the later corrected R3X1 values. These brackets are for zero-sign loss, not the **earlier** loss of the 1g requirement.

R3C1's discrete variational certificates were at approximately machine precision within the declared weighting. The direct full solve and Woodbury solve agreed at small residuals. Independent derivation and continuum error bounds still open.

### 14.2 R3D0 node-angle distinction

The old mirror's interface-weighted magnetic normal/tangent square fractions were approximately 0.5027061/0.4972939. Rotating \(\mathbf E\) near normal and \(\mathbf B\) near tangent is not free. Establishing the desired node requires approximately **45.15 degrees** gross reorientation in the diagnostic, whereas the additional node-centered angular variation was approximately **4.59 degrees**. These are separate requirements. Exact orthogonality can suppress \(E\cdot B\) itself, so an acceptable structure must retain sufficient \(\nabla(E\cdot B)\).

### 14.3 R3X2R2 scoped spectral limits

For a PSD source covariance \(Z\succeq0\), an acceleration form \(A_p\) at each sampled payload point, and positive canonical energy form \(H\), any payload-point probability weights \(\mu_p\) give

\[
\min_p a_p\ \le\ \sum_p\mu_p a_p
\ \le\ \lambda_{\max}\!\left[H^{-1/2}\left(\sum_p\mu_p A_p\right)H^{-1/2}\right]U_{\mathrm{field}}.
\]

The dual is an **upper bound** on the best achievable worst-point acceleration per unit canonical field energy. A negative upper eigenvalue excludes positive everywhere-outward acceleration within the PSD cone. A sufficiently small *positive* upper eigenvalue can exclude 1g at any source with canonical field energy below 10 MJ.

Key reproduced artifact entries at a declared normalized spectral perturbation of \(10^{-9}\):

| Family | Grid | Verified numerical inference |
|---|---|---|
| Shell-only \(\ell=1,2,3\) | 0.075 m | Conditional canonical-energy lower bound **27,708,418.061363768 J**. |
| Shell-only \(\ell=1,2,3\) | 0.050 m | Negative dual witness; no everywhere-outward configuration in that sampled PSD cone. |
| Shell + original baseline diagnostic | 0.075 m | **Not excluded** by the 10-MJ budget; conditional lower bound ~7.944 MJ. |
| Shell + original baseline diagnostic | 0.050 m | Conditional canonical-energy lower bound **25,466,113.6865739 J**. |

These are discrete, numerically reconstructed, conditional bounds. They do not include a certified input interval or continuum discretization error; the prescribed perturbation is a sensitivity test, not a proof that all physical model errors lie below it.

### 14.4 R3X3A opposite-sign cone

The signed two-sector relaxation reported a conditional canonical-energy lower bound **20,186,352.366549164 J** on the 0.075 m grid and a negative dual witness on the 0.050 m grid at the same declared \(10^{-9}\) perturbation. The resulting GREEN label is scoped to the ideal shell-source family. No rank-one constructive source surviving both grids was demonstrated.

## 15. Inverse-source audit — exact obligations of R3X3B0

### 15.1 Conjugacy identity

Let \(K_\phi,K_A\) be the static diagonal operators and \(C,D\) their corrected matter-induced coupling matrices. Under magnetic-source reversal,

\[
L_+=\begin{pmatrix}K_\phi&C\\-D&K_A\end{pmatrix},\quad
L_-=\begin{pmatrix}K_\phi&-C\\D&K_A\end{pmatrix},\quad
S=\operatorname{diag}(I,-I),\quad L_-=SL_+S.
\]

This identity is exact at the tested discrete operator level. It means changing the topological sign does not generate a genuinely new reachable response **if the allowed source domain is invariant under the corresponding magnetic-source reversal**. The microscopic realization of that source-domain symmetry is not certified; the identity must not be treated as a theorem about all physical two-field theories.

### 15.2 Exact preloaded field residual

For a fixed unloaded potential pair \((\phi_0,A_0)\), the extra right-hand-side needed to maintain *exactly the same potentials* under unit loaded coupling is

\[
\Delta j_\phi=C A_0,\qquad
\Delta j_A=-D\phi_0.
\]

This follows from subtracting unloaded from loaded discrete field equations; it does not require an optimizer and it is not evidence for a physically supplied current. A legitimate compensator must generate these Euler–Lagrange variations from **one gauge-invariant source/metric/matter action** with conserved total current, reciprocal matter forces, positive energy and acceptable support.

### 15.3 Source-support localization and decisive caveat

| Residual channel | Coarse fraction of residual L2 norm *inside payload* | Fine fraction |
|---|---:|---:|
| Static electric potential source | 93.58183458645922% | 98.27667736967488% |
| Azimuthal magnetic current | 88.74746712086443% | 93.78393023354690% |

Both channels had computed residual L2 fraction **1.0 outside the 2.0 m physical source support and the buffered 2.15/2.10 m supports**. The numerical sample says the *unchanged preloaded global fields* cannot be maintained by adjusting only the old external source currents in this model.

This is not a prohibition against a **changed** loaded field with the same everywhere-outward metric response, nor against new independent matter-polarization currents generated by a full action. One must also distinguish actual charge/current support from a discretization of a distributional boundary current.

### 15.4 Baseline source warning is separate and prior

R3X3B0 recorded L2 fractions for the **baseline electric-source operator residual** outside buffered support of **0.5477409167463158** (coarse) and **0.7089164053611221** (fine). Corresponding azimuthal magnetic baseline fractions were ~0.01233 and ~0.01616.

Before building an expensive compensator, determine whether this means:

1. the historical R3B target fields are only a global harmonic *inverse-design* construction without a source-confined physical realization;
2. boundary/interface distributions were spread or misclassified by the current finite-difference operators/masks;
3. the baseline-source operator used for the diagnostic is not identical to the variationally normalized Maxwell operator used by R3B;
4. outer boundary/truncation or source-work normalization introduced an artifact; or
5. there is a genuine uncompensated charge distribution outside the permitted support.

Do not declare the baseline globally unphysical without separating these alternatives. But **do not** claim a physically supported source until they are tested and resolved.

### 15.5 Quantitative source audit requirements

Independently reconstruct \(j_\phi^{(0)}=K_\phi\phi_0\), \(j_A^{(0)}=K_A A_0\), \(\Delta j_\phi=C A_0\), and \(\Delta j_A=-D\phi_0\) with the exact DOF masks and coordinate Jacobian. Report **weighted physical norms**, grid-volume norms, integrated net charge, current divergence, interface distributions, normal/tangential continuity, source-work identity and source-support leakage in cells/m²/m³ rather than just unweighted coefficient-vector L2 norms.

Do both grids with an independent differentiation implementation and verify that the physical support classification is stable against the chosen interface tolerance. A norm-fraction value alone does not establish total amount of charge or energy and must never be used as a source-energy estimate.

## 16. New physical-action construction — minimal common-action specification

### 16.1 Required field content and physical-metric map

The most economical serious candidate will contain two protected massless gauge sectors if needed by the cross-topological construction, universal physical matter metric, explicit microscopic source matter, and **at most one genuinely new interface degree of freedom/operator family at a time**. Reject a model that relies on independently set geometry, matter acceleration and carrier loading equations.

A schematic *candidate template*, **not an established viable Lagrangian**, is

\[
S=\int d^4x\sqrt{-g}\left[\frac{M_{\rm Pl}^2}{2}R-
\frac14 K_{IJ}(\chi,\varphi)F^I_{\mu\nu}F^{J\mu\nu}
-\frac12 Z_\chi(\partial\chi)^2-V(\chi)
+\mathcal L_{\rm src}(\Psi_s,A_I,g,\chi)
+\mathcal L_{\rm comp}(\chi,F_I,g)\right]
+S_m[\Psi_m,\tilde g_{\mu\nu}(g,F_I,\chi)] .
\]

Here \(I,J\) label independent U(1) fields; an action with fewer fields is preferred if it passes. Every function/operator is hypothetical until exact gauge, stability and empirical gates are satisfied. The chosen physical metric must be universal for neutral matter and must not secretly introduce species-dependent nonmetric forces.

### 16.2 Gauge and Noether ledger

For each gauge field, require the Euler–Lagrange equation, gauge identity, Bianchi identity, canonical current, and the corresponding matter-source Ward identity. A source current is allowed only if identically conserved or a Noether current of the **same action**. If there are chiral or axial source fields, require anomaly cancellation and a mass-generation mechanism compatible with the gauge symmetry.

For the combined system, derive the diffeomorphism Noether identity linking Einstein-frame stress-energy, physical-metric matter stress-energy, gauge dynamics and compensator equations. A local exchange of energy between matter and gauge fields is allowed; a violation of total conservation is not.

### 16.3 Matter action reciprocity is not optional

Under a universal conformal physical metric, the matter variation gives a term proportional to the physical trace of matter stress-energy times \(\delta\sigma\) (up to conventions and volume density factors). Derive the precise coefficient and sign directly from

\[
\delta S_m=\frac12\int d^4x\sqrt{-\tilde g}\,\tilde T^{\mu\nu}\delta\tilde g_{\mu\nu}.
\]

The mixed second variation of the same discrete/continuum action must satisfy reciprocal Hessian identities for physical source and matter variables. A compensating term cannot selectively cancel the gauge-sector response while leaving the metric force prescribed by hand; its contribution to **both** variations must be retained.

### 16.4 Topological, parity and CP gates

A single \(F\tilde F\) is a pseudoscalar; a conformal metric must transform appropriately under parity if parity is intended as a symmetry. Candidate choices include a parity-odd field or explicitly parity-violating action, but these choices impose distinct phenomenological constraints. An algebraic polar–axial assignment is **not** an anomaly-free microscopic UV theory. Record the exact transformation rules and whether parity/CP is preserved, broken explicitly, or broken spontaneously.

### 16.5 Field redefinition and genuinely independent source domains

Compute the full canonical kinetic matrix, its eigenvectors and all source couplings under any proposed \(F_X\tilde F_Y\) rewriting. Demonstrate with physical source restrictions and observables that the new completion is not related to R3X0/R3X3B0 by an invertible basis change. If it is, reject it as already tested. If the physical source constraints are different, **explicitly prove the difference** rather than infer it from new notation.

## 17. R3X3B1 — integrated theorem-first gate package

**Branch:** `032H17A12D1R3X3B1_ACTION_DERIVED_INTERFACE_COMPENSATOR_PREFLIGHT`

**Objective:** Determine whether a physically permitted new action term can change or compensate the original payload-interface response while retaining high metric leverage and strict source support. A pass is permission for a limited loaded BVP, **not** an antigravity physical-model certificate.

### Gate 0: frozen evidence and reproduction (mandatory)

- Verify historical R3B source implementation, original summary JSON and the field-globalization/multipole-fit artifacts.
- Record exact code and result file hashes, dependency versions, numerical precision, source-boundary convention, unit conversions and source-work normalization.
- Run historical control and corrected R3X3B0 control before any new science.
- Resolve or precisely classify the baseline electric source-support anomaly (§15.4); if not resolved, preserve it as a blocking physicalization warning.
- Reconstruct R3B's exact field-capacity number from a separate implementation or analytic multipole energy integration to independent tolerance.

**STOP:** If the source or invariant normalization cannot reproduce the benchmark, no compensator or numerical solver is authorized to promote the 1.54 J number. Do not change the historical claimed number silently; document a discrepancy.

### Gate 1: original action and field equations

- Write a single unambiguous Lorentzian action with physical/source/matter fields.
- Derive all Euler–Lagrange equations and conjugate source equations symbolically.
- Reproduce R3X1 constitutive signs and R3X3B0 exact inverse-source identities in the relevant limit.
- Verify the physical metric is universal and source and payload are not subject to hidden direct forces.

**STOP:** Wrong sign, action missing, different force/loading normalizations, or imposed counterterms with no action.

### Gate 2: conserved microscopic sources and geometric support

- Identify electric/magnetic source matter and its exact current(s), boundary surface terms and material interface stress.
- Determine whether the baseline fields can be sourced inside the original 2 m region and how the compensator changes source support.
- Quantify source inventory, confinement, charge neutrality, source work, current divergence and reaction force.

**STOP:** Required independent free charge/current only within the neutral payload when source confinement is mandatory, or unavoidable source/support lower bound already above 10 MJ.

### Gate 3: direct inverse-response matching

- Independently compute R3X3B0 \((\Delta j_\phi,\Delta j_A)\) with physically normalized source units.
- Compute the candidate operator's predicted variations for the same preloaded fields.
- Project the candidate response onto *both* interface residual channels, including normal and tangential components.
- Report remaining residual, net charge/current and full interface profile without using a freely chosen cancellation source.
- Determine whether exact original fields are necessary; allow a distinct loaded profile if the same-action metric response survives.

**STOP:** New operator only renames the old signed-sector source domain; or cancels portal gradient/metric acceleration at the same time as it cancels loading.

### Gate 4: gauge, Ward, Noether and Hessian reciprocity

- Check independent U(1) gauges and any compensator symmetry.
- Check source-current conservation and Bianchi identities exactly.
- Check diffeomorphism Noether energy-momentum exchange.
- Check equality of mixed action second derivatives after canonical weighting.
- Independently reconstruct the discrete variational operator, including boundary contributions.

**STOP:** Uncancelled anomaly, nonconserved source, nonreciprocal Hessian not explained by integration-by-parts/boundary terms, or wrong metric-variation force.

### Gate 5: spectrum, stability and canonical normalization

- Derive full quadratic fluctuation operator about the candidate background.
- Diagonalize kinetic and gradient matrices after removing gauge redundancies.
- Check ghost/gradient/tachyon health and the sign of energy in both field lanes and any compensator.
- Identify strong coupling/cutoff relative to the inverse-meter gradient and field amplitudes.
- Check nonaxisymmetric modes, source/cavity/interface motion and matter-gradient modes.

**STOP:** Unhealthy propagating modes, unprotected near-singular amplification, or fatal coupled instabilities.

### Gate 6: naturalness, parity/CP, anomaly and empirical exposure

- Determine parity/CP assignments and whether a pseudoscalar is required.
- If axial/chiral U(1) matter is present, calculate relevant gauge and mixed anomalies and their cancellation, plus mass compatibility.
- Analyze loop-generated operators, photon mixing, ordinary-matter coupling and technical naturalness at the 1-keV reference portal normalization.
- Match exact physical operator to existing inverse-square-law, equivalence-principle, electromagnetic-environment, spin/axion and astrophysical constraints. Do not import mismatched bounds as conclusions.

**STOP:** Quantitative excluded prediction, fatal radiative term, or inconsistent UV/mass-generation hypothesis.

### Gate 7: minimal complete-energy feasibility bound

- Lower-bound material/source, field, compensator, confinement, support, polarization, activation, control, reset, reaction/radiation and vacuum/off-state contributions.
- Distinguish field-only inventory from physically conservative complete operating energy.
- Propagate uncertainties and use strict \(<10\,000\,000\ \mathrm J\) rule.

**STOP:** Any unavoidable nonnegative component \(\ge10\) MJ, even if Maxwell field energy is 1.54 J. If only loose lower bounds are available, label feasibility unknown rather than green.

### Gate 8: conditional BVP authorization

A loaded finite-payload BVP is justified only if Gates 1–7 contain no fatal failure and the new physical response is genuinely different from the closed families. Require identical physical source coefficients on all grids, direct full sparse solves against the independent reduction, gauge and source residual checks, full 1 kg payload, true 1 m gap, and worst-point tracking. A favorable relaxation without a constructive rank-one source is **not** sufficient.

### Single-run decision labels

```text
RED_ACTION_OR_WARD_NO_GO = DO_NOT_RUN_BVP
RED_SOURCE_SUPPORT_NO_GO = STOP_THIS_SOURCE_FAMILY
RED_HEALTH_OR_EMPIRICAL_NO_GO = STOP_THIS_REALIZATION
RED_FIELD_ENERGY_LOWER_BOUND_GE_10MJ = STOP_BUDGET_REALIZATION
YELLOW_NUMERICAL_OR_SOURCE_PROVENANCE_UNRESOLVED = REPAIR_CERTIFICATE
YELLOW_ACTION_SURVIVES_SOURCE_UNPHYSICALIZED = ADVANCE_ONLY_PHYSICAL_SOURCE
GREEN_PREFLIGHT_SAME_ACTION_CONSTRUCTIVE_POTENTIAL = AUTHORIZE_ONE_LOADED_BVP
```

A `GREEN_PREFLIGHT` is *not* proof of an antigravity model. The final program success gate remains far downstream.

## 18. Candidate ordering for a genuinely new interface mechanism

Do not launch an indiscriminate operator scan. Prioritize distinct candidate **actions** by how directly they address the measured interface residual and how little unverified supporting physics they require.

### 18.1 First: geometric-node / independent source-domain operator

Investigate whether a local gauge-invariant coupling depending on the **actual dynamical matter or compensator field** modifies the constitutive response such that destructive \(\nabla T\)-dependent terms are reduced while useful \(\nabla(E\cdot B)\) remains. The operator must transform as a scalar and produce all backreaction terms from the same action. The original single-field exact node and sign-only two-field relabelings remain closed.

An interface-localized coupling explicitly depending on \(T\) must be derived in a correct EFT/matter action; ordinary local dependence on an external prescribed density field is insufficient. If the proposed density is itself a dynamical fluid, include its stress-energy and conservation constraints.

### 18.2 Second: dynamical pseudoscalar compensation

A term schematically \(\chi F_X\tilde F_Y\) changes the effective topological coefficient only if \(\chi\) has a physical spatial profile and solves its **own** field equation. Include \(\frac12(\partial\chi)^2\), the potential \(V(\chi)\), its source, all mixed variations and the complete energy cost. Avoid assuming free cancellation, a massless chiral source, or protected low mass without a symmetry argument.

### 18.3 Third: cross-kinetic/polarizable response

A dynamical mixing or material polarization \(f(\chi,\Psi_m)F_X^{\mu\nu}F_{Y\mu\nu}\) can change the constitutive matrix but generally adds new bulk loading and stress-energy. It must be tested with the **physical** matter sector and basis-invariant kinetic eigenvalues. Reject if it merely recreates the old quadratic \(F^2\) catastrophe or creates new nonmetric forces.

### 18.4 Fourth: new protected geometric mediator

A genuinely distinct gauge/metric/torsion or curvature-mediated coupling may alter the matter-gradient interface physics but introduces healthy-mode, Ward, naturalness and empirical obligations. Prefer theoretical no-go/yes-go preflight, not another blind BVP. V26D and other historical geometric models remain knowledge, not an excuse to reopen closed forms.

### 18.5 Structural gate across all families

Only a candidate that changes an observable invariant **and** the constrained physical source manifold is worth a new expensive solve. A cheaper field-capacity term by itself is not a reason to prioritize it.

## 19. Vacuum, dilute payload and smooth-interface branch — diagnostic, not device shortcut

### 19.1 What empty vacuum does and does not mean

An electric/magnetic field can exist in an evacuated active region with physical sources on or outside its boundaries. In an ideal matter-free region, the matter-trace source of the specific R3 topological loading may vanish locally. A preexisting field gradient may therefore be consistent with the reduced *vacuum* equations.

**But the program requires repulsion on finite neutral matter.** The moment a 1 kg payload enters, its own trace/density gradients, spatial interfaces and stress-energy variation reappear. Evacuating air does not remove the payload's self-backreaction. Chamber walls, electrodes, magnet supports and motion systems also constitute material source structures and must ultimately be accounted for.

### 19.2 Clean test-body-limit experiment

In the same action, define a sequence of physically meaningful stress-energy profiles \(T_{\mu\nu}^{(\eta)}\), approaching zero as \(\eta\to0^+\), and solve the **same fully coupled field equations**. Keep portal coefficients and sources fixed; never rescale only the matter loading while prescribing the old metric force as an independent law.

Record the vector/source response, worst-point acceleration, COM acceleration, field inventory, total stress-energy normalization, source leakage and solver residual. A successful infinitesimal test-body limit is a separate result and does not fulfill the 1 kg reference target.

### 19.3 Fixed-mass smooth-interface control

A physically consistent comparison at fixed 1 kg may redistribute density gradients but cannot simply delete them. Normalize total mass, preserve finite material support and stand-off, include material equation of state and stress, and integrate gradient-layer energy/support. Do not infer success from a lower peak \(\lvert\nabla T\rvert\) alone; integrated interface effects can remain large.

### 19.4 Hollow payload / double-boundary control

A vacuum cavity can displace material interfaces from the highest-leverage field region, but it also introduces an inner interface. Check both boundaries, source stand-off measured to the **nearest** source material and any hidden internal support. A hollow payload still needs real mass and realistic stress-energy.

### 19.5 Time-dependent preload and insertion

If the fields are preloaded in vacuum before a neutral object enters, the transient response must be determined by the causal time-dependent same-action field equations. Initial apparent force is not conservative stand-off. Include radiation, reaction, field switching, control power and reset. Do not claim that delaying equilibration avoids reciprocal matter loading.

### 19.6 Vacuum gate outcomes

```text
EMPTY_VACUUM_FIELD_CAPACITY_ONLY = DOES_NOT_MEET_FINITE_PAYLOAD_FLOOR
DILUTE_TEST_BODY_ACCELERATION = RESEARCH_RESULT_ONLY
FIXED_1KG_SMOOTH_INTERFACE = REQUIRES_FULL_LOADED_BVP
VACUUM_CHAMBER_SOLVES_1KG_BACKREACTION = UNPROVEN
VACUUM_ENERGY_OR_ZERO_POINT_HARVESTING = NOT_THE_R3B_MODEL
```

Vacuum/dilute tests should follow or accompany the action provenance preflight only if their cost is small and they answer a distinct causal question. They are not a replacement for R3X3B1.

## 20. Full numerical verification and continuum promotion standard

### 20.1 Codes and artifacts

A science branch must have separate numerical implementation, simulation entry point, targeted tests, immutable JSON summary, logs and previous-state comparison. Use the source/codebundle **as implemented**, not a manually reconstructed formula inconsistent with its operator weighting.

### 20.2 Initial regression integrity

Last **recorded** successful full suite: **1,283 passed** from R3X3B0. This is not a fresh run for the present document and does not certify newer source edits. Record source revision/hash, Python/library versions and test discovery count. Do not use an old 1,080-test baseline as current.

### 20.3 Convergence

For every survivor, compare both original grids and a new demonstrably refined independent grid, with outer-domain and source-support sensitivity. In particular, worst-point acceleration is strongly grid-sensitive and **not converged** merely because one field-energy quantity remains near 1.5 J. Track spatial movement of the worst payload point, interface eigenvectors, local metric gradient and conserved source flux.

### 20.4 Direct independent solve

Use at least two independent numerical reconstruction paths where possible: full coupled sparse solve versus Woodbury reduction, analytic multipole energy versus discrete field integral, and analytic action/Hessian differentiation versus finite differences or automatic differentiation. A shared-code numerical check is only partially independent.

### 20.5 Source-support weighting

Use physical cylindrical-axisymmetric measures \(2\pi\rho\,d\rho\,dz\), correct grid Jacobians and boundary measure. Verify masks align with actual DOFs. An ordinary L2 norm of a coefficient vector is **not** a conserved charge, current magnitude, mechanical reaction, or energy.

### 20.6 Symmetry and spectrum

Check Lorentzian-sign-correct constitutive blocks, gauge Bianchi, exact Ward, massless gauge-null directions, source current divergence and action-based weighted reciprocity. A positive Maxwell Gram matrix is necessary but not sufficient for stability of coupled matter/compensator and gravity modes.

### 20.7 Numerical certificate discipline

A declared spectral perturbation such as \(10^{-9}\) is not a formal bound on continuum discretization or uncertain physical inputs. To claim an actual **certified** no-go, establish operator interval bounds, mesh error controls and physical source-family exhaustion. A solver status alone is never a theorem.

### 20.8 No false success by rank relaxation

A PSD covariance cone contains configurations that need not factor into a single physical field/vector per sector. Positive SDP margin is only an upper-level necessary/relaxed feasibility test; extract rank-one realizations, check signed source amplitudes, support, same coefficients across grids and full independent loaded BVP before promotion.

## 21. Full physical certification stack and stop conditions

| Gate | Required evidence | Stop / hold condition |
|---|---|---|
| Genuine source | Explicit finite microscopic charge/current and support | Reconstructed current located only inside nominally neutral payload or outside permitted source domain |
| Same action | Unified Einstein/metric/matter/gauge/compensator equations | Hand-imposed force and independent loading rules |
| Ward/Noether | Exact conserved current, gauge identities and total stress-energy exchange | Uncancelled source divergence, wrong reciprocity, anomaly |
| Canonical health | Positive physical spectrum after removing gauge modes | Ghost/gradient/tachyon/strong-coupling collapse |
| Universal physical metric | One composition-independent metric for ordinary neutral matter | Special direct payload charge, fifth-force relabeling |
| True finite payload | Acceleration \(\ge9.80665\) m/s² everywhere in finite 1 kg neutral torus | Local or COM only, wrong sign anywhere |
| Stand-off | Nearest source-to-payload separation \(\ge1\) m, no embedded source | Hidden electrodes/support in the payload or gap |
| Source reaction | Explicit conservation of momentum and support loads | Reactionless claim, unbalanced force |
| Numerical convergence | Independent source/current, field-energy and worst-point convergence | Sign flip or unstable interface error under refinement |
| Nonlinear consistency | Full metric/matter/source backreaction | Linear preloaded response only |
| Empirical consistency | Correct operator-to-experiment matching | Strong existing bounds exclude necessary coupling |
| UV/naturalness | Radiative correction, mixing, cutoff and symmetry analysis | Fine-tuning beyond stated allowed tolerance / invalid EFT |
| Complete energy | Every unavoidable positive operating energy component, strict <10 MJ | Any mandatory term \(\ge10\) MJ or unknown large scaffolding |
| Device claim | All prior gates + experimental demonstration | No physical apparatus or measurement |

The first gate that fails for a **specific realization** closes that realization, not the entire universe of untested theories. The energy ceiling excludes any candidate for which one unavoidable nonnegative component itself costs \(\ge10\) MJ. An upper bound on a partial cost or a conditional field-capacity number never certifies a complete budget.

---

## 22. Complete operating ledger — mandatory classification

Every reported energy quantity must have one of these explicit ledger types:

```text
FIELD_ONLY
PRELOADED_REDUCED_MODEL_CAPACITY
SOURCE_ONLY
SOURCE_PLUS_FIELD
MICROSCOPIC_PARTIAL
PARTIAL_OPTIMISTIC_LOWER_BOUND
PARTIAL_CONSERVATIVE_LOWER_BOUND
COMPLETE_STATIC
COMPLETE_OPERATING
```

Eventual conservative complete-energy ledger includes:

\[
E_{\mathrm{complete}}=E_{\mathrm{source}}+E_{\mathrm{field}}+E_{\mathrm{metric}}+E_{\mathrm{comp}}+E_{\mathrm{support}}+E_{\mathrm{confinement}}+E_{\mathrm{polarization}}+E_{\mathrm{activation}}+E_{\mathrm{control}}+E_{\mathrm{reset}}+E_{\mathrm{reaction}}+E_{\mathrm{radiation}}+E_{\mathrm{backreaction}}+E_{\mathrm{quantum/UV}}+E_{\mathrm{leakage}}+E_{\mathrm{offstate}}.
\]

Avoid double counting energy components that overlap; define a single system boundary and thermodynamic protocol. But also avoid ignoring field preparation, structural rest mass, compensator gradients or off-state power by treating them as someone else's cost.

Until the full ledger is derived from a physical model, `COMPLETE_OPERATING_ENERGY = UNKNOWN`, no matter how small the field-only quantity is. **Exactly 10 MJ fails.** A mandatory conservative lower bound at or above that threshold closes practical certification for the corresponding realization.

## 23. Historical source and operator atlas: current interpretation

The September A12D0 atlas represented **12 source families, 9 field-sector portals, 78 combinations and 35 historically open combinations**. Those numbers are historical structural bookkeeping, **not** the count of presently viable physical sources. The minimal electron Pauli/magnetization path and the original ordinary \(F^2\) reuse have since been tested and closed in their practical proposed forms.

Keep distinct surviving *classes*, subject to new physics and their own exact gate: composite atomic/nuclear sources, source-selective full Noether vector completions, interacting/bound-state Dirac, flavor-selective currents, pseudotensor derivative currents, composite geometric/curvature source currents, neutrino currents only after power preflight, other electrostatic/disformal/parity-odd/derivative or multiple-field portals. Do not treat a named class as physically feasible merely because a prior source atlas did not falsify it.

Preserve A12D0's proven **local algebraic one-Abelian-field quadratic metric basis** within its stated assumptions: parity-even rank-two metric structures built from \(g_{\mu\nu}F^2\) and \(F_{\mu\alpha}F_\nu{}^\alpha\), parity-odd mixed structures reduce to conformal forms. This does **not** exhaust multiple-field, derivative, curvature, higher-order or dynamical compensator-dependent portals.

Pauli/minimal electronic magnetization is **not** the R3X3B1 next step. Preserve its exact failure evidence in the latest journal and AGMINER memory. No broad source atlas reset.

## 24. Permanent historical GR, geometric-source and HOOK17 anchors

### 24.1 006D versus B7/026

006D: strongest project conservative true-external-stand-off linearized-GR result, \(C=23.591586299249\), finite positive energy, local conservation, NEC/WEC/DEC and positive far-field active mass, but catastrophic approximate \(1/G\) practicality scaling. B7/026: strongest historical microscopic false-core GR morphology, principally embedded/two-sided and requiring separate convergence duties; not a successor to 006D. 026C N=89 remains a historical credibility issue, not today's highest-value task.

### 24.2 018B and 018C

018B field existence survives its original scoped proof. 018C fatal \(m=2\) instability remains a permanent warning that static source existence does not imply interacting stability. Protected-vector closures through 022A remain closed absent genuinely new symmetry.

### 24.3 Introspective

Preserve exactly `RAW_GE1000X_SIGNAL=PRESENT` and `CERTIFIED_GE1000X_CONTINUUM_SOURCE=NO`. The strong positive lesson is compact productive, low-cancellation, payload-adjacent high-leverage fields with high canonical response per joule and low scaffolding. The negative lesson is that a raw numerical signal is not a continuum physical source.

## 27. New-run protocol — fast but source-faithful

1. Read the current codebundle, latest artifacts and signed November journal; confirm the historical record still agrees with actual source code.
2. Verify the suite against **1,283 last recorded passes**, unless a newer source snapshot legitimately changes count; explain any mismatch.
3. State one precise physical action and one exact *reason it could differ* from the closed R3 field-basis families.
4. State the cheapest decisive falsifier before coding.
5. Check gauge identities, microscopic source support, field-redefinition invariance and reciprocal matter action **before** expensive BVP.
6. Check energy lower bounds and empirical contradictions before parameter scans.
7. If the candidate survives, solve one finite-payload BVP with identical source parameters and independent sparse/direct validation across grids.
8. Only if finite-payload \(\ge1g\) and \(\ge1m\) survive: test interacting stability, nonlinear/quantum naturalness and full energy.
9. Stop at the first fatal failure; record what was falsified and what remains untested.
10. On closeout, update one journal entry and chronology note; regenerate code/result bundles; update `RESEARCH_BUILDPLAN.md` and README only for material state changes.

The user prefers complete, paste-ready shell blocks in the chat for science runs; do not create downloadable script files unless requested. Do not use version-control shell commands in run instructions. Preserve old artifact files and old code; use new run IDs for all new outputs.

## 28. Scientific promotion and reporting vocabulary

Every branch should select exactly one **claim class** with explicit scope:

| Label | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `ALGEBRAIC_IDENTITY` | Symbolic relation; no sourced field or payload proof. |
| `PRELOADED_REDUCED_MODEL_CAPACITY` | Specified field/metric response without complete reciprocal matter solution. |
| `GLOBAL_FIELD_LEVEL_COMPLETION` | Boundary-completed field with specified mathematical neutrality; physical source may be open. |
| `SAME_ACTION_LOADED_FINITE_GRID` | Coupled source and matter field equations checked on sampled meshes. |
| `SCOPED_DUAL_NUMERICAL_EXCLUSION` | Valid witness for a stated discrete source cone and tolerance only. |
| `ACTION_DERIVED_SOURCE_PREFLIGHT` | Candidate exact source/action identities passed; full BVP not yet passed. |
| `HEALTHY_MODE_REALIZATION` | Proper gauge-fixed fluctuation spectrum healthy; other physical gates may fail. |
| `FINITE_PAYLOAD_STANDOFF` | Everywhere-outward on finite neutral matter with true gap in actual loaded solution. |
| `FULL_NONLINEAR_CONSISTENT_MODEL` | Full coupled action, conservation, source and stability checks. |
| `COMPLETE_SUB10MJ_CERTIFIED` | Complete conservative operating ledger strictly below 10 MJ, all prior gates passed. |
| `PHYSICAL_DEVICE_DEMONSTRATED` | Reproducible experiment with measured gravitational response and full system. |

R3B presently belongs to `PRELOADED_REDUCED_MODEL_CAPACITY` with a field-level global completion; R3X3B0 is a `SAME_ACTION_LOADED_FINITE_GRID` *falsification* and exact inverse-support obstruction, not a successful physical model. 006D remains `CONSERVATIVE_LINEARIZED_GR_TRUE_STANDOFF_ANCHOR` only within its declared linearized approximation.

No project section may label these as equivalent achievements.

## 29. Strict reproducibility and publication readiness protocol

### 29.1 Source identity and hashes

Preserve historical file spellings (including `prefight`) and store cryptographic hashes for each implementation, simulation, test, input artifact and output artifact. A historically reported checksum is not verified against an unpinned later extract; report any mismatch instead of silently changing the expected source. The September codebundle may not contain November modules.

### 29.2 Fresh clean-environment reproduction

For full independent confidence, a second researcher/process should reconstruct the R3B field equation, multipoles, 1g normalization and canonical field integral without importing the source routines that produced the initial number. Recompute energy on finer meshes and an independent exterior integral; establish error bars instead of advertising all printed digits as significant.

### 29.3 Variational consistency

Independently vary source, matter and any compensator from the same action. Compare symbolic derivative, automatic derivative and finite differences with normalization and boundary terms. Check full Ward identities and source-work consistency, not just an isolated Maxwell residual.

### 29.4 Artifact checks

Compare the historical R3B summary and newest R3X3B0 JSON against expected keys and numerical fields; verify both stand-off geometry and payload masks. Do not fabricate a missing JSON field by inference or accept a printed number that is absent from the structured artifact.

### 29.5 Precision acceptance

Distinguish approximate reproducibility (`1e-8` J regression tolerance, for example) from mathematically certified continuum accuracy. Establish minimum-payload acceleration margins **above** exactly 1g when possible so discretization error cannot silently invalidate the everywhere-outward claim.

### 29.6 Trust boundaries

Research artifacts, narrative interpretations and software tests are not experimentally established physical phenomena. README/tweet language must identify reduced-model status and the loaded sign failure. No collaborator should infer a working 1.54 J apparatus.
